                                                                                                                    1. A compliance finding is a gap between required controls and observed evidence, not merely an unfavorable dashboard color.
                                                                                                                    2. Copilot DLP must use supported scenario-specific conditions and actions; it does not automatically govern every agent interaction.
                                                                                                                    3. A guest can participate through a governed external identity; external chat capability is not equivalent to membership or file access.
                                                                                                                    Answer and explanation

                                                                                                                    1 → A; 2 → C; 3 → B

                                                                                                                    Agent compliance gaps: A compliance finding is a gap between required controls and observed evidence, not merely an unfavorable dashboard color. Guests and external collaboration: A guest can participate through a governed external identity; external chat capability is not equivalent to membership or file access. Copilot DLP boundaries: Copilot DLP must use supported scenario-specific conditions and actions; it does not automatically govern every agent interaction. The distinction is the purpose and scope of each control, not a shared product label.

                                                                                                                    Q290 — Case review — constructed response

                                                                                                                    L58 | D3.G1.3

                                                                                                                    Northstar change review: An agent handles regulated records but has no documented owner review or tested retention requirements. A colleague proposes: Close the finding because the agent has no recent errors. In 100–150 words, explain why this does or does not meet the requirement; recommend a scoped correction, describe outcome evidence, and identify a rollback or limitation. Do not invent an observed successful test.

                                                                                                                      Answer and explanation

                                                                                                                      Rubric: requirement 1; correction 1; rejection rationale 1; verification 1; rollback/limitation 1.

                                                                                                                      Model response: Reliability does not prove compliance. Record the specific gaps, assign accountable remediation and require evidence before closing them. A compliance finding is a gap between required controls and observed evidence, not merely an unfavorable dashboard color. Verify as follows: Link each requirement to a configured control, tested outcome, owner and unresolved limitation. Record the baseline and reverse only the disposable scoped change if acceptance fails; for irreversible, retention-protected or chargeable operations, state the limitation and obtain the normal owner decision before action. Award the final point only for a limitation or rollback relevant to this case, not a generic claim that everything can be undone.

                                                                                                                      Q291 — Scenario single choice

                                                                                                                      L59 | D3.M1.1

                                                                                                                      A Microsoft 365 AI report shows 1200 currency units, including 300 also shown in an Azure view for the same metered use. Which action best meets the requirement?

                                                                                                                      1. Report prompt count as currency
                                                                                                                      2. Ignore all Azure costs
                                                                                                                      3. Reconcile the common charge identifiers and period, then count the overlapping 300 only once.
                                                                                                                      4. Add both totals as independent charges
                                                                                                                      Answer and explanation

                                                                                                                      C

                                                                                                                      A: Incorrect. Usage and financial measures require a defined conversion. B: Incorrect. Some separate charges may genuinely exist. C: Correct. Cost reporting needs consistent periods, billing boundaries and units; overlapping views must not be added as separate charges. D: Incorrect. That double-counts the same metered use.

                                                                                                                      Q292 — Evidence single choice

                                                                                                                      L59 | D3.M1.1

                                                                                                                      After addressing this case: A Microsoft 365 AI report shows 1200 currency units, including 300 also shown in an Azure view for the same metered use. Which evidence is strongest for closing the change?

                                                                                                                      1. One pilot user reports that the general experience looks normal.
                                                                                                                      2. The administrator can complete a similar task with an elevated account.
                                                                                                                      3. The change ticket is approved and the configuration page shows Saved.
                                                                                                                      4. Produce a reconciled ledger with unique charges, overlaps, excluded taxes or credits and the reporting period.
                                                                                                                      Answer and explanation

                                                                                                                      D

                                                                                                                      A: Incorrect. A general report lacks the specific resource, identity, action and expected negative test needed to validate this requirement. B: Incorrect. An administrator may have different access and policy scope from the actual pilot identity; this is not a representative outcome test. C: Incorrect. Authorization and saved configuration do not prove that the intended operation reached the right objects or was enforced. D: Correct. This tests the stated control at the relevant scope and outcome.

                                                                                                                      Q293 — Multiple response — choose two

                                                                                                                      L59 | D3.M1.1

                                                                                                                      2. Ignore all Azure costs
                                                                                                                      3. Produce a reconciled ledger with unique charges, overlaps, excluded taxes or credits and the reporting period.
                                                                                                                      4. Reconcile the common charge identifiers and period, then count the overlapping 300 only once.
                                                                                                                      5. Add both totals as independent charges
                                                                                                                      Answer and explanation

                                                                                                                      C, D

                                                                                                                      A: Incorrect. Usage and financial measures require a defined conversion. B: Incorrect. Some separate charges may genuinely exist. C: Correct. This supplies the control-specific verification required before claiming success. D: Correct. Cost reporting needs consistent periods, billing boundaries and units; overlapping views must not be added as separate charges. E: Incorrect. That double-counts the same metered use.

                                                                                                                      Answer and explanation

                                                                                                                        Q296 — Scenario single choice

                                                                                                                        L60 | D3.M1.2;D3.M1.3;D3.M1.4

                                                                                                                        There are 100 licensed users, 40 active users and a suspected service incident. Leadership asks whether the rollout is successful. Which action best meets the requirement?

                                                                                                                        1. Report the defined 40 percent active-license rate, investigate service health separately and collect task-quality evidence before claiming value.
                                                                                                                        2. Call 100 purchased seats 100 percent adoption
                                                                                                                        3. Infer a service outage solely from low usage
                                                                                                                        4. Treat a healthy service dashboard as proof users gain value
                                                                                                                        Answer and explanation

                                                                                                                        A

                                                                                                                        A: Correct. Usage, useful outcomes and service reliability are different measures. Healthy adoption reporting defines the population and time window. B: Incorrect. Licensing is not activity. C: Incorrect. Other causes such as training and workload fit remain possible. D: Incorrect. Availability is not business benefit.

                                                                                                                        Q299 — Matching / drag-and-drop equivalent

                                                                                                                        L60 | D3.M1.2;D3.M1.3;D3.M1.4

                                                                                                                        Match each concept to its description. Use every answer once. Concepts: 1. Adoption and AI service health; 2. Privileged roles and PIM; 3. DLP alert response

                                                                                                                        1. A DLP alert needs context about policy, item, actor and action before a responder can judge exposure and tune controls.

DLP alert response: A DLP alert needs context about policy, item, actor and action before a responder can judge exposure and tune controls. The distinction is the purpose and scope of each control, not a shared product label.

                                                                                                                          Q301 · D1 · L02

                                                                                                                          Northstar has verified a new domain using the requested TXT record. Its mailboxes are still hosted by another provider. Which observation best confirms that verification has not changed mail delivery?

                                                                                                                          1. A new employee can sign in with a temporary address.
                                                                                                                          2. The domain appears in the Microsoft 365 domain list.
                                                                                                                          3. The MX record still points to the approved existing mail provider.
                                                                                                                          4. The TXT verification record is visible.
                                                                                                                          Answer and explanation

                                                                                                                          C. MX records direct inbound mail. The other observations concern verification or identity and do not establish the mail route.

                                                                                                                          Review lesson L02

                                                                                                                          Q302 · D1 · L04

                                                                                                                          A new employee is in the correct licensing group but has an assignment error showing an invalid usage location. What should you correct first?

                                                                                                                          1. The user's usage location, then recheck the assignment result.
                                                                                                                          2. The group's display name, then wait for a report refresh.
                                                                                                                          3. The employee's mailbox delegation, then retry sign-in.
                                                                                                                          4. The tenant's branding, then remove all licence groups.
                                                                                                                          Answer and explanation

                                                                                                                          A. The error identifies an assignment prerequisite. Changing unrelated group presentation, mailbox permissions or branding does not resolve it.

                                                                                                                          Review lesson L04

                                                                                                                          Q303 · D1 · L06

                                                                                                                          Finance asks for proof that a particular deleted document can be recovered to a usable state. Which evidence most directly meets the requirement?

                                                                                                                          1. A screenshot of the Backup setup page.
                                                                                                                          2. A permitted restore of that document followed by a content check.
                                                                                                                          3. A retention policy that includes the finance site.
                                                                                                                          4. An invoice showing that Backup has been paid for.
                                                                                                                          Answer and explanation

                                                                                                                          B. A restore plus verification tests the actual outcome. Retention, configuration and payment alone do not demonstrate a usable recovery.

                                                                                                                          Review lesson L06

                                                                                                                          Q304 · D1 · L07

                                                                                                                          Copilot works from home but fails for several users at one office. What comparison would best narrow the next investigation?

                                                                                                                          1. The users' licence purchase dates alone.
                                                                                                                          2. The number of SharePoint sites in each user's favourites.
                                                                                                                          3. The office's current and previous company logos.
                                                                                                                          4. Affected and unaffected network paths for the same service and period.
                                                                                                                          Answer and explanation

                                                                                                                          D. A location-specific pattern supports comparing connection paths. The other comparisons do not directly test the observed difference.

                                                                                                                          Review lesson L07

                                                                                                                          Q305 · D1 · L09

                                                                                                                          A support worker can read the shared mailbox but receives an error when sending as its address. Which permission should you inspect?

                                                                                                                          1. The team's owner role.
                                                                                                                          2. SharePoint site collection administrator.
                                                                                                                          3. Full Access only, because it includes every sending right.
                                                                                                                          4. Send As for the shared mailbox.
                                                                                                                          Answer and explanation

                                                                                                                          D. Reading and sending as a mailbox are distinct permissions. Full Access alone does not establish Send As, and the other roles govern different resources.

                                                                                                                          Review lesson L09

                                                                                                                          Q306 · D1 · L10

                                                                                                                          An employee belongs to a team but cannot see a private channel used by its finance subgroup. What should you check first?

                                                                                                                          1. Whether the employee can create a public team.
                                                                                                                          2. Whether the employee can send from the shared mailbox.
                                                                                                                          3. Whether the employee is a member of that private channel.
                                                                                                                          4. Whether the tenant has a verified email domain.
                                                                                                                          Answer and explanation

                                                                                                                          C. Private-channel membership is narrower than team membership. The other conditions do not establish access to that channel.

                                                                                                                          Review lesson L10

                                                                                                                          Q307 · D1 · L13

                                                                                                                          A user has a saved document link and valid SharePoint permission. The site now has Restricted Content Discovery. What is the expected direct-access implication?

                                                                                                                          1. RCD alone does not revoke the user's existing direct access.
                                                                                                                          2. The saved link is automatically revoked.
                                                                                                                          3. The document is deleted from the source site.
                                                                                                                          4. The user's permission becomes edit-only.
                                                                                                                          Answer and explanation

                                                                                                                          A. RCD addresses broad discovery. It does not revoke links, rewrite permission levels or delete source content.

                                                                                                                          Review lesson L13

                                                                                                                          Q315 · D2 · L17

                                                                                                                          A user is eligible for a PIM-managed role but cannot perform the privileged task yet. What distinction should you investigate?

                                                                                                                          1. A mail contact versus a distribution list.
                                                                                                                          2. Eligibility versus an active role assignment.
                                                                                                                          3. A private channel versus a meeting transcript.
                                                                                                                          4. A licence invoice versus a usage report.
                                                                                                                          Answer and explanation

                                                                                                                          B. PIM eligibility may require activation before the role is usable. The other distinctions do not explain this privileged-role state.

                                                                                                                          Review lesson L17

                                                                                                                          Q316 · D2 · L18

                                                                                                                          A helpdesk role is scoped to an administrative unit containing selected users. Which statement is justified?

                                                                                                                          1. The helpdesk automatically becomes administrator of every workload.
                                                                                                                          2. Supported directory tasks can be delegated for that scope; this is not a universal file-access boundary.
                                                                                                                          3. The administrative unit creates a separate Microsoft 365 tenant.
                                                                                                                          4. Every file owned by those users becomes inaccessible to everyone else.
                                                                                                                          Answer and explanation

                                                                                                                          B. Administrative units scope supported administration. They do not create tenants, isolate every file or automatically grant all workload administration.

                                                                                                                          Review lesson L18

                                                                                                                          Q317 · D2 · L19

                                                                                                                          A supplier needs to appear in the address book but will not sign in or access company files. Which object best fits?

                                                                                                                          3. A privileged employee account.
                                                                                                                          4. An agent identity blueprint.
                                                                                                                          Answer and explanation

                                                                                                                          Review lesson L19

                                                                                                                          Q318 · D2 · L20

                                                                                                                          2. The required Graph permissions, consent and applicable role or scope for that operation.

                                                                                                                          Q319 · D2 · L21

                                                                                                                          An authentication method is enabled for the pilot group, but a user has not registered it. What does enabling the policy establish?

                                                                                                                          1. The user has satisfied every Conditional Access policy.
                                                                                                                          2. The method is allowed for the targeted user; registration and use still need verification.
                                                                                                                          3. The user's password has been reset.
                                                                                                                          4. The user has already used the method successfully.
                                                                                                                          Answer and explanation

                                                                                                                          B. Method policy, registration and event-level use are separate states. Enabling a method does not prove reset, successful use or all access requirements.

                                                                                                                          Review lesson L21

                                                                                                                          Q320 · D2 · L23

                                                                                                                          Q321 · D2 · L25

                                                                                                                          Two enforced Conditional Access policies apply. One requires MFA; the other blocks access. The user satisfies MFA. What happens?

                                                                                                                          1. The policies cancel each other and access is allowed.
                                                                                                                          2. The applicable block still denies access.
                                                                                                                          3. The MFA success overrides the block.
                                                                                                                          4. Whichever policy was created most recently wins.
                                                                                                                          Answer and explanation

                                                                                                                          B. An applicable block takes precedence. Conditional Access is not resolved by creation order, cancellation or an MFA override.

                                                                                                                          Review lesson L25

                                                                                                                          Q322 · D2 · L24

                                                                                                                          A user signs in without a fresh MFA prompt. Which evidence should you inspect before claiming a bypass?

                                                                                                                          1. Only the tenant's active-user count.
                                                                                                                          2. The sign-in's authentication details and results of applicable policies.
                                                                                                                          3. Only the user's profile photograph.
                                                                                                                          4. Only the number of available MFA methods.
                                                                                                                          Answer and explanation

                                                                                                                          B. An existing claim or session may satisfy MFA. The specific event and policy results establish what occurred; counts and profile appearance do not.

                                                                                                                          Review lesson L24

                                                                                                                          Q323 · D2 · L30

                                                                                                                          A malicious message was delivered, but there is no evidence yet that the recipient clicked or disclosed credentials. Which incident statement is accurate?

                                                                                                                          1. All three events are confirmed by delivery.
                                                                                                                          2. No investigation is necessary because a click is unconfirmed.
                                                                                                                          3. Delivery is confirmed; click and compromise require further evidence.
                                                                                                                          4. The recipient's device is definitely compliant.
                                                                                                                          Answer and explanation

                                                                                                                          C. Evidence should distinguish delivery, interaction and compromise. Missing click evidence does not justify closing the investigation or making device claims.

                                                                                                                          Review lesson L30

                                                                                                                          Q324 · D2 · L33

                                                                                                                          A sensitivity label is published to a pilot group. A learner says every old document is now encrypted. What is wrong with the claim?

                                                                                                                          1. Published labels can never protect documents.
                                                                                                                          2. Publishing a label is the same as deleting unlabelled documents.
                                                                                                                          3. Publishing makes the label available; application and configured protection must be checked separately.
                                                                                                                          4. Only a domain-verification record can enable encryption.
                                                                                                                          Answer and explanation

                                                                                                                          C. Label availability, label application and encryption configuration are distinct. Labels can protect content when configured, but publishing alone is not blanket encryption.

                                                                                                                          Review lesson L33

                                                                                                                          Q325 · D2 · L34

                                                                                                                          4. Only the Copilot licence group.

                                                                                                                          Q342 · D3 · L44

                                                                                                                          A permitted user can open a source file directly but cannot find it through Copilot Search. What is the best next investigation?

                                                                                                                          1. Remove all sensitivity labels without checking their effect.
                                                                                                                          2. Search configuration and source discovery or indexing, while preserving the existing access boundary.

                                                                                                                          Answer and explanation

                                                                                                                          Q343 · D3 · L46

                                                                                                                          An administrator blocks the legacy Cowork agent entry but leaves the user in a current spending policy selecting Cowork. What must be reviewed to remove access?

                                                                                                                          1. Only whether web citations appear in one response.
                                                                                                                          2. All applicable Cowork spending-policy access scopes and prerequisites.
                                                                                                                          3. Only the label on a SharePoint document.
                                                                                                                          4. Only the old agent entry again.
                                                                                                                          Answer and explanation

                                                                                                                          B. Current Cowork access uses spending-policy scope. The legacy entry and web behaviour do not establish revocation.

                                                                                                                          Review lesson L46

                                                                                                                          Q344 · D3 · L47

                                                                                                                          A federated connector retrieves from its source at query time without indexing source content in Microsoft 365. Which statement remains true?

                                                                                                                          1. An indexed ACL must be the only authorisation mechanism.
                                                                                                                          2. Every source user automatically has access to every record.
                                                                                                                          3. No information ever leaves the source.
                                                                                                                          4. Requested information can still be transferred during the query.
                                                                                                                          Answer and explanation

                                                                                                                          D. No index does not mean no runtime data transfer. Federated retrieval still needs the appropriate source authorisation.

                                                                                                                          Review lesson L47

                                                                                                                          Q345 · D3 · L50

                                                                                                                          An agent's business sponsor leaves the company. What should the lifecycle process ensure?

                                                                                                                          1. Changing the registry title updates every owner automatically.
                                                                                                                          2. The agent continues indefinitely with no accountable person.
                                                                                                                          3. Accountability is reassigned and relevant ownership and access are reviewed.
                                                                                                                          4. The sponsor's former personal password is retained for continuity.
                                                                                                                          Answer and explanation

                                                                                                                          C. Sponsorship should remain current. Registry presentation does not transfer all responsibilities, and retaining a departed person's password is not an appropriate identity design.

                                                                                                                          Q346 · D3 · L51

                                                                                                                          A policy targets a blueprint's child agent identities. What should you avoid assuming?

                                                                                                                          1. That policy scope matters.
                                                                                                                          2. That the same policy automatically targets each paired agent user account.
                                                                                                                          3. That fresh relevant sign-in evidence can help verification.
                                                                                                                          4. That an agent identity and a paired user account are distinct objects.
                                                                                                                          Answer and explanation

                                                                                                                          B. The paired user account is a separate subject. Scope and sign-in evidence remain important.

                                                                                                                          Q347 · D3 · L54

                                                                                                                          An agent publication request has no identified owner and asks for more tool access than its task needs. What is the defensible decision?

                                                                                                                          1. Treat a publication request as automatic consent to every permission.
                                                                                                                          2. Return or reject it until ownership and least-privilege requirements are resolved.
                                                                                                                          3. Assign every user as owner and skip the review.
                                                                                                                          4. Publish immediately because its description sounds useful.
                                                                                                                          Answer and explanation

                                                                                                                          B. Purpose, accountability and needed capabilities must be established. A description or request is not blanket approval.

                                                                                                                          Review lesson L54

                                                                                                                          Q348 · D3 · L57

                                                                                                                          An agent is approved for one data source. An activity review shows attempted use of a different sensitive source. What should you investigate?

                                                                                                                          1. Only the agent's icon.
                                                                                                                          2. The agent identity, tool, permission route and actual result against its approved purpose.
                                                                                                                          3. Only whether the sponsor likes the interface.
                                                                                                                          4. Only total tenant storage capacity.
                                                                                                                          Answer and explanation

                                                                                                                          B. The relevant evidence concerns the actor, capability, resource and result. Appearance, preference and aggregate storage do not establish authorised behaviour.

                                                                                                                          Review lesson L57

                                                                                                                          Q349 · D3 · L59

                                                                                                                          A report shows 120 licensed users and 72 active users in the same period. What is active adoption among licensed users?

                                                                                                                          1. 48%.
                                                                                                                          2. 60%.
                                                                                                                          3. 120%.
                                                                                                                          4. 72%.
                                                                                                                          Answer and explanation

                                                                                                                          B. 72 divided by 120 is 0.60. The denominator is licensed users, not 100; 48 is the count of licensed users not active in that period, not the requested percentage.

                                                                                                                          Q308 · D1 · L03

                                                                                                                          An administrator updates an organisation privacy setting. The change ticket has approval and the portal says Saved. What remains necessary before declaring the desired behaviour achieved?

                                                                                                                          1. Treat the save confirmation as an end-to-end test.
                                                                                                                          2. Verify the effective behaviour at the setting's actual scope after propagation.
                                                                                                                          3. Change every service setting to match the new value.
                                                                                                                          4. Make the ticket approver a Global Administrator.
                                                                                                                          Answer and explanation

                                                                                                                          B. A saved configuration is not proof of the resulting experience. Extra privilege or unrelated changes do not provide that evidence.

                                                                                                                          Q309 · D1 · L05

                                                                                                                          A maker can author an AI solution, but the intended user cannot run the funded experience. Which review best addresses this without assuming one permission covers everything?

                                                                                                                          1. Rename the solution so its funding becomes active.
                                                                                                                          2. Increase the maker's administrative role and assume the user inherits it.
                                                                                                                          3. Check user entitlement, maker permission and runtime funding separately.
                                                                                                                          4. Use the maker's success as proof of every user's access.
                                                                                                                          Answer and explanation

                                                                                                                          C. Authoring permission, product entitlement and funding answer different questions. Neither names nor another identity's result establish the user's prerequisites.

                                                                                                                          4. Only one user's recent files list.

                                                                                                                          2. Only that a team name includes the word Copilot.
                                                                                                                          3. The effective Copilot meeting policy and the required transcription behaviour for that experience.
                                                                                                                          4. That every attendee has mailbox Full Access.

Meeting Copilot and transcript behaviour depend on the applicable meeting configuration and prerequisites. Unrelated ownership, names and mailbox delegation do not establish them.

                                                                                                                          Q312 · D1 · L12

                                                                                                                          A site owner removes one direct permission, but the user still opens the file through a group. What is the most accurate conclusion?

                                                                                                                          1. Another effective permission route remains and must be reviewed.
                                                                                                                          2. SharePoint ignores all permission changes.
                                                                                                                          3. The user must be a tenant administrator.
                                                                                                                          4. A retention label has granted the user read access.
                                                                                                                          Answer and explanation

                                                                                                                          A. Removing one route does not remove a separate group grant. The facts do not establish a platform failure, a retention-based grant or an administrator role.

                                                                                                                          Q313 · D1 · L13

                                                                                                                          A site uses Restricted Access Control. Priya is in an allowed group but has no underlying SharePoint permission. What should the access test show?

                                                                                                                          1. Access allowed because the allowed group grants Read automatically.
                                                                                                                          2. Access allowed only through Copilot.
                                                                                                                          3. Access allowed if the link is marked view-only.
                                                                                                                          4. Access denied until both required conditions are satisfied.
                                                                                                                          Answer and explanation

                                                                                                                          D. RAC requires allowed-group membership and underlying permission. It does not grant Read, and Copilot or a link label cannot bypass the requirement.

                                                                                                                          2. The tenant's subscription name proves all resources are protected.
                                                                                                                          3. The required recovery is not established by the available backup evidence.
                                                                                                                          4. A backup of another site proves this site is covered.
                                                                                                                          Answer and explanation

                                                                                                                          Q327 · D2 · L14

                                                                                                                          A staff member moves from finance to sales. What should the access review address?

                                                                                                                          1. Change only the user's display name and declare the move complete.
                                                                                                                          2. Keep all old access permanently because the account is unchanged.
                                                                                                                          3. Remove unjustified old access and assign only the new role's required access.
                                                                                                                          4. Delete every finance document the person previously opened.
                                                                                                                          Answer and explanation

                                                                                                                          C. Access should follow the person's current responsibilities. The same account does not justify old privileges; deleting documents or changing a name is not an access review.

                                                                                                                          4. That guest users are identities.
                                                                                                                          Answer and explanation

                                                                                                                          Q329 · D2 · L16

                                                                                                                          A manager wants a group for a shared collaboration workspace. Another request is only for authorising access to a resource. How should you choose group types?

                                                                                                                          1. Treat all group types as interchangeable.
                                                                                                                          2. Match the group type to the collaboration or access purpose and supported workload.
                                                                                                                          3. Choose whichever group name is shortest.
                                                                                                                          4. Use a mail contact for both requests.
                                                                                                                          Answer and explanation

                                                                                                                          B. Group types have different capabilities. Names do not establish those capabilities, and a contact is not a group.

                                                                                                                          Q331 · D2 · L25

                                                                                                                          A report-only Conditional Access block policy shows a matching failure, while the sign-in succeeds. Is that necessarily a policy malfunction?

                                                                                                                          1. Yes; report-only must block matching requests.
                                                                                                                          2. No; report-only evaluates without enforcing that block.
                                                                                                                          3. Yes; successful sign-ins never appear in logs.
                                                                                                                          4. No; block policies never enforce access decisions.
                                                                                                                          Answer and explanation

                                                                                                                          B. The policy state explains this result. Report-only does not enforce, but an enforced applicable block can deny access.

                                                                                                                          Q332 · D2 · L26

                                                                                                                          An analyst confuses user risk with sign-in risk. Which distinction is useful?

                                                                                                                          1. Possible account compromise versus risk attached to a particular sign-in.
                                                                                                                          2. Neither can inform an access-policy design.
                                                                                                                          3. User risk is a licence count; sign-in risk is a billing amount.
                                                                                                                          4. Both mean a confirmed successful attack in every case.
                                                                                                                          Answer and explanation

                                                                                                                          A. The two risk signals have different subjects. They are not billing measures or automatic proof of a successful attack and can inform supported policies.

                                                                                                                          3. That all threat policies should be deleted.
                                                                                                                          4. Which policy actually applies to that recipient and feature.
                                                                                                                          Answer and explanation

                                                                                                                          D. Effective protection depends on applicability and precedence. Existence alone is insufficient; renaming or deleting everything is not a diagnosis.

                                                                                                                          Q334 · D2 · L31

                                                                                                                          An organisation wants staff to recognise and report phishing. Which outcome best evaluates a training campaign's behavioural purpose?

                                                                                                                          1. Only whether the campaign was given an attractive title.
                                                                                                                          2. Relevant reporting and response behaviour, interpreted with the campaign context.
                                                                                                                          3. A claim that the simulation proves all real attacks are stopped.
                                                                                                                          4. Only the number of licence invoices generated.
                                                                                                                          Answer and explanation

                                                                                                                          B. The desired outcome is behaviour. Invoices and titles do not measure it, and a controlled exercise cannot prove universal protection.

                                                                                                                          Q335 · D2 · L32

                                                                                                                          A detector matches the intended synthetic sensitive record and also a harmless near-match. What should you do?

                                                                                                                          1. Assume all future content is correctly classified.
                                                                                                                          2. Delete the near-match result from the evidence without explanation.
                                                                                                                          3. Investigate the false positive and tune or justify the classification design.
                                                                                                                          4. Claim 100% precision because one intended match was found.
                                                                                                                          Answer and explanation

                                                                                                                          C. The near-match reveals a detection-quality issue. Keep the evidence, investigate it and avoid unsupported performance claims.

                                                                                                                          Q336 · D2 · L35

                                                                                                                          A Copilot prompt DLP policy is in simulation. A matching event appears. What can you conclude?

                                                                                                                          1. All agent interactions are now covered by the same policy.
                                                                                                                          2. Every uploaded file was scanned by the prompt text control.
                                                                                                                          3. The activity matched the simulated policy; this does not establish blocking.
                                                                                                                          4. The user was definitely blocked by that policy.
                                                                                                                          Answer and explanation

                                                                                                                          C. Simulation establishes a match, not enforcement. Do not infer broader upload scanning or universal agent coverage from that event.

                                                                                                                          3. Only the fact that an administrator edited it.
                                                                                                                          4. Only the total number of users in the tenant.
                                                                                                                          Answer and explanation

                                                                                                                          B. A change record is not outcome evidence. Test the affected scenario and document what was confirmed.

                                                                                                                          Q338 · D2 · L38

                                                                                                                          A data-risk dashboard identifies an overshared site. What is the appropriate next step?

                                                                                                                          1. Treat the dashboard finding itself as completed remediation.
                                                                                                                          2. Buy more licences and assume sharing changes automatically.
                                                                                                                          3. Hide the report while retaining all excessive access.
                                                                                                                          4. Assign an owner, inspect the access problem, remediate and verify.
                                                                                                                          Answer and explanation

                                                                                                                          D. A finding identifies work; it does not perform it. Licensing or hiding evidence does not correct the access boundary.

                                                                                                                          2. Only whether the answer is long.
                                                                                                                          3. Whether the answer accurately reflects the cited source and the task.
                                                                                                                          4. Only whether the source has a short filename.
                                                                                                                          Answer and explanation

                                                                                                                          C. A citation supports traceability, not automatic correctness. Length and filenames do not establish accurate interpretation.

                                                                                                                          3. Delete the risk entry but keep the same permissions.
                                                                                                                          4. Expand immediately because useful drafts cancel security findings.
                                                                                                                          Answer and explanation

                                                                                                                          Q357 · D3 · L53

                                                                                                                          1. They require review; discovery does not itself establish approval or appropriate access.
                                                                                                                          2. Their source permissions are necessarily identical.
                                                                                                                          3. Every discovered agent is automatically approved.
                                                                                                                          4. All third-party agents are confirmed malicious.
                                                                                                                          Answer and explanation

                                                                                                                          A. Inventory is evidence of existence, not approval, maliciousness or a uniform permission model.

                                                                                                                          Q358 · D3 · L55

                                                                                                                          An agent is hidden from a catalogue. What separate question remains before claiming its underlying integration is fully revoked?

                                                                                                                          1. Whether another unrelated agent was published.
                                                                                                                          2. Whether the agent has a shorter description.
                                                                                                                          3. Whether relevant identities, credentials, tools and source access still permit operation.
                                                                                                                          4. Whether the catalogue has a new colour theme.
                                                                                                                          Answer and explanation

                                                                                                                          C. Visibility and operational authorisation differ. Review the relevant underlying paths rather than cosmetic or unrelated changes.

                                                                                                                          Q359 · D3 · L56

                                                                                                                          An agent dashboard has no events, but collection scope and report delay have not been checked. What is the accurate finding?

                                                                                                                          1. All monitoring is definitely broken.
                                                                                                                          2. The agent definitely performed no operations.
                                                                                                                          3. The agent is therefore compliant with every requirement.
                                                                                                                          4. Insufficient evidence to conclude that the agent had no activity.
                                                                                                                          Answer and explanation

                                                                                                                          D. Absence of displayed events can have several causes. Verify scope and availability before concluding no activity, compliance or a collection failure.

                                                                                                                          Q360 · D3 · L60

                                                                                                                          An adoption dashboard and a cost report disagree in their totals. What should you compare first?

                                                                                                                          1. Only the alphabetical order of user names.
                                                                                                                          2. Reporting period, population, metric definition, delay and billing coverage.
                                                                                                                          3. Assume usage events and billable units must always be identical.
                                                                                                                          4. Only the colours of the charts.
                                                                                                                          Answer and explanation

                                                                                                                          B. The reports may measure different things. Align definitions and scope before judging a discrepancy; usage is not automatically a charge unit.
